# FOREMAN_REPORT

**PARTIAL — source work is implemented. The required regression criterion is not met in this sandbox.**

Implemented additive authority/Fee source and Core bindings, generated disbursement constraints, and an offline SDK preparation consumer. Retained bytes are intact. Full requested regression verification is blocked by sandbox EPERM. This is not full lifecycle ledger correspondence or acceptance.

**Generator conflict and resolution:** Use additive custody/generate-lifecycle.mjs. Keep custody/generate.mjs, loan.compact and swap.compact byte-identical. The new emitter is the sole source for loan-lifecycle.compact. This deliberately differs from the requested generate.mjs edit because that edit invalidates a retained receipt that this round cannot regenerate.

An initial generate.mjs extension produced stale build sources failures in ledger, compiled-rejection and finalized-state suites. Restoring its original bytes removed those failures. Both failed and repaired outputs are retained.

## Model

Codex, based on GPT-6. I cannot verify the exact served model ID because this host exposes no response model metadata. The supplied runtime identity is Codex based on GPT-6.

## Versions and bindings

- financial agreement source: `moriarty-financial-agreement-source/5` → `moriarty-financial-agreement-source/6`. Existing /5 factory, frontend and source bytes unchanged.
- financial expression Core: `moriarty-financial-expression-contract/4` → `moriarty-financial-expression-contract/5`. Existing /4 preserved. Fee uses Emit with operation Fee. No new expression constructor.
- lifecycle state: `moriarty-financial-lifecycle-state/1` → `moriarty-financial-lifecycle-state/2`. /1 remains accepted by the unchanged legacy entry. /6 requires /2 and rejects downgrade.
- lifecycle kernel input/result: `moriarty-financial-lifecycle/1` → `moriarty-financial-lifecycle/2`. Original kernel entry remains unchanged. New /2 entry admits authority and Fee.

financial-lifecycle-v2.ts adds required authority network/program commitments, debtor/lender parties and capability hashes, and outcome intent. Role mismatches reject. Successful state preserves bindings. Local admission is not cryptographic consent. SDK preparation checks actual Compact persistentHash against supplied read commitments before creating private arguments.

Only `custody/loan-lifecycle.compact`, circuit `originate`, now asserts lender capability with `LENDER_CAPABILITY`. It also asserts borrower capability with `BORROWER_CAPABILITY`. Actor IDs are lender 5 and borrower 2. The artifact is generated by `custody/generate-lifecycle.mjs` and is uncompiled. The retained loan circuits are unchanged.

## Fees

`checked(credits) >= checked(debits + minimumLedgerAmount)`.

Transfer and Fee sum per actor/asset in UInt128 ledger quanta. Self effects count both sides. Missing sums are zero. Nonmonetary effects count neither. Every debit requires a gross cap, even zero. Refunds do not reduce gross debit. Overflow rejects. Fee never creates repayment or origination credit.

createFinancialAgreementSourceV6 -> Core /5 -> prepareStagedLifecycleKernel in funded-expression-source-v2.ts -> lifecycle /2 -> checkOutcomeIntent. The SDK caller invokes the same source evaluator. The generated originate circuit independently checks in-asset gross caps and net goals.

IN-ASSET fees only. fees.netGoal.assetMixing remains OPEN for DUST/SPECK. Source Amount<Cash> and lifecycle amounts denote ledger quanta. SDK supports explicit identity quantum 1. Exact conversion helper rejects nondivisibility and UInt128 intermediate overflow. fees.dustFeeCap.value remains OPEN. No value invented.

## Existing expectations

No existing test or expectation was edited, weakened, skipped, deleted or renamed. Only new test files were added. The two K failures and tests/lifecycle-k-corpus.test.mjs remain untouched.

## Gap 4 — explicit open items

NO. A complete ledger schema, accepted-state projection, persistent transition updates and compiled rejection evidence are required. Compilation is prohibited this round.

The retained loan continues to rely on revision and the kernel cursor alone. It has no on-ledger allowance or used-identifier registers.

The new disbursement-only wrapper uses revision plus a one-shot originated flag. This does not implement any of the four used-identifier registers.

- **OPEN `state.allowances.ledgerRepresentation`:** On-ledger allowance remaining/spent registers and complete accepted-read mapping are not implemented. Transaction intent caps in the new wrapper do not replace lifecycle allowances.
- **OPEN `state.replayHistory.ledgerRepresentation`:** On-ledger usedTransferIds, usedAllocationIds, usedOriginationIds and usedAccrualIds are not implemented. Complete persistence and rejection evidence require a later ledger-schema change.

## Limitations and open work

- The new generator accepts only the exact selected source digest. It rejects source changes, including added guards. It is not a general source-to-Compact compiler.
- The wrapper does not store full financial lifecycle state. Source obligations retain principal, accrued and outstanding fields. Principal was never missing.
- The original private staged interpreter files remain frozen. New versioned runtime forks duplicate code and need synchronized future bug review.
- SDK tests use the installed real Compact persistentHash runtime with labeled synthetic inputs and a fake createUnprovenCallTx capture. No real SDK transaction was constructed or submitted.
- The SDK copies mutable head, role and asset inputs before asynchronous runtime loading. Secret argument arrays stay in a closure.
- Fee amount disclosure is explicit before public unshielded ledger effects. Compilation remains unperformed.

- **OPEN `fees.netGoal.assetMixing`:** Fee-inclusive means IN-ASSET economic fees only. DUST/SPECK does not enter Cash net goals. No conversion is defined or invented.
- **OPEN `fees.dustFeeCap.value`:** No native fee cap value is supplied or invented.
- **OPEN `authority.identities.lifecycleRunReuse`:** Offline source work does not establish reuse or continued availability of any live identities or secrets.
- **OPEN `authority.admission.current`:** No admission or accounting record was created or changed. Guarded status could not open its SQLite database.
- **OPEN `state.fieldMapping.sourceToLedger`:** The new wrapper is an authority/fee disbursement artifact. It does not implement complete obligation, balance, interest, time-cursor or source-state projection.
- **OPEN `state.thirdParty.otherToken`:** Source /2 preserves unrelated balances and allowances. The specialized Cash wrapper has no on-ledger Other/Token projection.
- **OPEN `failure.deferredLedgerObligations`:** Capabilities authenticate possession of role secrets. Native debit payer attribution and full consumed-input ownership remain unproved.
- **OPEN `task5.2a.compilation`:** The new wrapper was generated but never compiled. No generated runtime rejection, deployment, proving, or network result is claimed.
- **OPEN `sdk.compiledAndReadProvenance`:** The SDK caller compares supplied read bindings and prepares unproven-call arguments. The enclosing launcher must authenticate reads and establish compiled-contract provenance.
- **OPEN `verification.sandbox`:** Cannot satisfy the required exactly-two-language-failures and zero-ledger-failures criterion in this sandbox. Node spawnSync returns EPERM. Full nested counts show 52 additional CLI failures and one ledger FIFO failure.
- **OPEN `review.requiredProviders`:** No fresh Opus/Astra provider approval is claimed. One internal Codex subagent supplied bounded expected cases and source findings. Offline instructions precluded provider network dispatch.

I cannot satisfy the complete verification requirement in this sandbox: Node spawnSync returns EPERM, causing existing CLI and FIFO tests to fail.
I cannot verify the exact served model ID because this host exposes no response model metadata. The supplied runtime identity is Codex based on GPT-6.
I did not implement full on-ledger lifecycle state or authenticated read/compiled-contract provenance. These remain explicit open items.

All edits remain in the worktree. No git write, prohibited-path edit, artifact rebuild, compiler invocation, Docker start, submission, installation, accounting mutation, or roadmap checkbox change occurred.
